Changes against main b165d1d:
- Image 39932105942 (f234b8e): the main image plus the sm90-decode-independent-wg host patch, which takes effect only when DG_SM90_DECODE_INDEPENDENT_WG=1 and the shape is the Qwen Decode SwapAB geometry.

- The Decode worker exports DG_SM90_DECODE_INDEPENDENT_WG=1, and the startup line prints it. The serving numeric gate runs with the same env, so the gate checks the path Decode actually takes.
- Unchanged: the FA3 verify and dense in-job selection, cap 128, concurrency 192, hot-4 Prefill replication and EAGLE3 steps 3 / draft 4.

Also in this branch:
- Codex's unpushed main commits: the MegaMoE numeric-gate reference fixes, the stricter kernel-bench acceptance, the Firmus build-only tag route and the source review notes.
- --megamoe-only for the kernel-bench gate.
- The removal of the stale patches/sglang-bench copies.
- The kernel-bench results appended to research/runtime-performance/2026-10-23-decode-source-review.md.
The full qwen-experiment-metrics suite, ruff and the line-continuation check pass locally.
